#### Building filter modules｜建構過濾模組

When writing a body or header filter, pay special attention to the filter's position in the filter order. There's a number of header and body filters registered by nginx standard modules. The nginx standard modules register a number of head and body filters and it's important to register a new filter module in the right place with respect to them. Normally, modules register filters in their postconfiguration handlers. The order in which filters are called during processing is obviously the reverse of the order in which they are registered.

編寫請求體或請求頭過濾器時，請特別注意過濾器在過濾器順序中的位置。 nginx 標準模組註冊了許多請求頭和請求體過濾器。這些標準模組註冊了一系列請求頭和請求體過濾器，因此，註冊新的過濾器模組時，請務必確保其位置與現有過濾器相對應。通常，模組會在其配置後處理程序中註冊過濾器。過濾器在處理過程中被呼叫的順序顯然與它們的註冊順序相反。

For third-party filter modules nginx provides a special slot `HTTP_AUX_FILTER_MODULES`. To register a filter module in this slot, set the `ngx_module_type` variable to `HTTP_AUX_FILTER` in the module's configuration.

對於第三方過濾器模組，nginx 提供了一個特殊的插槽`HTTP_AUX_FILTER_MODULES` 。若要在此插槽中註冊過濾器模組，請在模組的配置中將`ngx_module_type`變數設定為`HTTP_AUX_FILTER` 。

The following example shows a filter module config file assuming for a module with just one source file, `ngx_http_foo_filter_module.c`.

以下範例顯示了一個過濾器模組配置文件，假設該模組只有一個來源文件， `ngx_http_foo_filter_module.c` 。

> ```
> ngx_module_type=HTTP_AUX_FILTER
> ngx_module_name=ngx_http_foo_filter_module
> ngx_module_srcs="$ngx_addon_dir/ngx_http_foo_filter_module.c"
> 
> . auto/module
>
> . car/模組
> ```

#### Buffer reuse｜緩衝區重複使用

When issuing or altering a stream of buffers, it's often desirable to reuse the allocated buffers. A standard and widely adopted approach in nginx code is to keep two buffer chains for this purpose: `free` and `busy`. The `free` chain keeps all free buffers, which can be reused. The `busy` chain keeps all buffers sent by the current module that are still in use by some other filter handler. A buffer is considered in use if its size is greater than zero. Normally, when a buffer is consumed by a filter, its `pos` (or `file_pos` for a file buffer) is moved towards `last` (`file_last` for a file buffer). Once a buffer is completely consumed, it's ready to be reused. To add newly freed buffers to the `free` chain it's enough to iterate over the `busy` chain and move the zero size buffers at the head of it to `free`. This operation is so common that there is a special function for it, `ngx_chain_update_chains(free, busy, out, tag)`. The function appends the output chain `out` to `busy` and moves free buffers from the top of `busy` to `free`. Only the buffers with the specified `tag` are reused. This lets a module reuse only the buffers that it allocated itself.

當發出或變更緩衝區流時，通常需要重複使用分配的緩衝區。 nginx 程式碼中廣泛採用的標準方法是為此目的保留兩個緩衝鏈：`free` 和 `busy`。 `free` 鏈保留所有空閒緩衝區，可重複使用。 `busy` 鏈保留目前模組發送的所有緩衝區，這些緩衝區仍在被其他過濾器處理程序使用。如果緩衝區的大小大於零，則認為該緩衝區正在使用。通常，當過濾器消耗緩衝區時，其`pos`（或對於檔案緩衝區為`file_pos`）將移向`last`（對於檔案緩衝區為`file_last`）。一旦緩衝區被完全消耗，就可以重新使用了。要將新釋放的緩衝區添加到`free`鏈，只需迭代`busy`鏈並將其頭部的零大小緩衝區移動到`free`即可。這個操作很常見，所以有一個特殊的函數`ngx_chain_update_chains(free, busy, out, tag)`。此函數將輸出鏈`out`附加到`busy`，並將空閒緩衝區從`busy`頂部移動到`free`。僅重複使用指定 `tag` 的緩衝區。這使得模組僅重複使用它自己分配的緩衝區。

The following example is a body filter that inserts the string “foo” before each incoming buffer. The new buffers allocated by the module are reused if possible. Note that for this example to work properly, setting up a [header filter](#http_header_filters) and resetting `content_length_n` to `-1` is also required, but the relevant code is not provided here.

以下範例是一個正文過濾器，它會在每個傳入緩衝區之前插入字串「foo」。模組分配的新緩衝區會盡可能重複使用。請注意，要使此範例正常運作，還需要設定 [header filter](#http_header_filters)並將`content_length_n`重設為`-1` ，但此處未提供相關程式碼。

> ```
> typedef struct {
>     ngx_chain_t  *free;
>
>     ngx_chain_t *free;
>     ngx_chain_t  *busy;
>
>     ngx_chain_t *busy;
> }  ngx_http_foo_filter_ctx_t;
>
> } ngx_http_foo_filter_ctx_t;
> 
> 
> ngx_int_t
> ngx_http_foo_body_filter(ngx_http_request_t *r, ngx_chain_t *in)
> {
>     ngx_int_t                   rc;
>
>     ngx_int_t rc;
>     ngx_buf_t                  *b;
>
>     ngx_buf_t *b;
>     ngx_chain_t                *cl, *tl, *out, **ll;
>
>     ngx_chain_t *cl, *tl, *out, **ll;
>     ngx_http_foo_filter_ctx_t  *ctx;
>
>     ngx_http_foo_filter_ctx_t *ctx;
> 
>     ctx = ngx_http_get_module_ctx(r, ngx_http_foo_filter_module);
>     if (ctx == NULL) {
>
>     如果 (ctx == NULL ) {
>         ctx = ngx_pcalloc(r->pool, sizeof(ngx_http_foo_filter_ctx_t));
>         if (ctx == NULL) {
>
>         如果 (ctx == NULL ) {
>             return NGX_ERROR;
>
>             返回 NGX_ERROR；
>         }
> 
>         ngx_http_set_ctx(r, ctx, ngx_http_foo_filter_module);
>     }
> 
>     /* create a new chain "out" from "in" with all the changes */
>
>     /* 從“in”建立新的鏈“out”，並將所有變更套用到“in”中 */
> 
>     ll = &out;
> 
>     for (cl = in; cl; cl = cl->next) {
> 
>         /* append "foo" in a reused buffer if possible */
>
>         /* 如果可能，將「foo」追加到重複使用的緩衝區中 */
> 
>         tl = ngx_chain_get_free_buf(r->pool, &ctx->free);
>         if (tl == NULL) {
>
>         如果 (tl == NULL ) {
>             return NGX_ERROR;
>
>             返回 NGX_ERROR；
>         }
> 
>         b = tl->buf;
>         b->tag = (ngx_buf_tag_t) &ngx_http_foo_filter_module;
>         b->memory = 1;
>         b->pos = (u_char *) "foo";
>         b->last = b->pos + 3;
> 
>         *ll = tl;
>         ll = &tl->next;
> 
>         /* append the next incoming buffer */
>
>         /* 追加下一個傳入的緩衝區 */
> 
>         tl = ngx_alloc_chain_link(r->pool);
>         if (tl == NULL) {
>
>         如果 (tl == NULL ) {
>             return NGX_ERROR;
>
>             返回 NGX_ERROR；
>         }
> 
>         tl->buf = cl->buf;
>         *ll = tl;
>         ll = &tl->next;
>     }
> 
>     *ll = NULL;
>
>     *ll = NULL ;
> 
>     /* send the new chain */
>
>     /* 發送新鏈 */
> 
>     rc = ngx_http_next_body_filter(r, out);
> 
>     /* update "busy" and "free" chains for reuse */
>
>     /* 更新「忙碌」和「空閒」鏈以便重複使用 */
> 
>     ngx_chain_update_chains(r->pool, &ctx->free, &ctx->busy, &out,
>                             (ngx_buf_tag_t) &ngx_http_foo_filter_module);
> 
>     return rc;
>
>     返回 rc；
> }
> ```

#### Load balancing｜負載平衡

The [ngx\_http\_upstream\_module](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md>) provides the basic functionality needed to pass requests to remote servers. Modules that implement specific protocols, such as HTTP or FastCGI, use this functionality. The module also provides an interface for creating custom load-balancing modules and implements a default round-robin method.

[ngx_http_upstream_module](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md>)提供將請求傳遞給遠端伺服器所需的基本功能。實作特定協定（例如HTTP或 FastCGI）的模組會使用此功能。該模組還提供了一個用於創建自訂負載平衡模組的接口，並實現了預設的輪詢方法。

The [least\_conn](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#least_conn>) and [hash](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#hash>) modules implement alternative load-balancing methods, but are actually implemented as extensions of the upstream round-robin module and share a lot of code with it, such as the representation of a server group. The [keepalive](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#keepalive>) module is an independent module that extends upstream functionality.

[least\_conn](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#least_conn>)和 [hash](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#hash>)模組實現了不同的負載均衡方法，但實際上是作為上游輪詢模組的擴展而實現的，並與其共享大量程式碼，例如伺服器群組的表示。 [keepalive](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#keepalive>)模組是一個獨立的模組，用於擴展上游功能。

The [ngx\_http\_upstream\_module](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md>) can be configured explicitly by placing the corresponding [upstream](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#upstream>) block into the configuration file, or implicitly by using directives such as [proxy\_pass](<085 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_proxy_module.md#proxy_pass>) that accept a URL that gets evaluated at some point into a list of servers. The alternative load-balancing methods are available only with an explicit upstream configuration. The upstream module configuration has its own directive context `NGX_HTTP_UPS_CONF`. The structure is defined as follows:

可以透過在設定檔中放置對應的 [upstream](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#upstream>)程式碼區塊來明確設定 [ngx\_http\_upstream\_module](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md>) ，也可以透過使用諸如 [proxy\_pass](<085 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_proxy_module.md#proxy_pass>)之類的指令來隱式配置，這些指令接受一個URL參數，該參數最終會被解析為伺服器列表。其他負載平衡方法僅在明確配置上游模組時可用。上游模組配置有其自身的指令上下文`NGX_HTTP_UPS_CONF` 。其結構定義如下：

> ```
> struct ngx_http_upstream_srv_conf_s {
>     ngx_http_upstream_peer_t         peer;
>
>     ngx_http_upstream_peer_t peer;
>     void                           **srv_conf;
>
>     void **srv_conf;
> 
>     ngx_array_t                     *servers;  /* ngx_http_upstream_server_t */
>
>     ngx_array_t *servers; /* ngx_http_upstream_server_t */
> 
>     ngx_uint_t                       flags;
>
>     ngx_uint_t 標誌；
>     ngx_str_t                        host;
>
>     ngx_str_t host;
>     u_char                          *file_name;
>
>     u_char *file_name;
>     ngx_uint_t                       line;
>
>     ngx_uint_t 線；
>     in_port_t                        port;
>
>     輸入埠_t 埠;
>     ngx_uint_t                       no_port;  /* unsigned no_port:1 */
>
>     ngx_uint_t no_port; /* unsigned no_port:1 */
> 
> #if (NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_ZONE)
>     ngx_shm_zone_t                  *shm_zone;
>
>     ngx_shm_zone_t *shm_zone;
> #endif
> };
> ```

-   `srv_conf` — Configuration context of upstream modules.  
    `srv_conf` — 上游模組的配置上下文。
-   `servers` — Array of `ngx_http_upstream_server_t`, the result of parsing a set of [server](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#server>) directives in the `upstream` block.  
    `servers` — `ngx_http_upstream_server_t`數組，解析`upstream`塊中的一組 [server](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#server>)指令的結果。
-   `flags` — Flags that mostly mark which features are supported by the load-balancing method. The features are configured as parameters of the [server](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#server>) directive:  
    `flags` — 這些標誌主要用於標記負載平衡方法支援哪些功能。這些功能配置為 [server](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#server>)指令的參數：
    -   `NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_CREATE` — Distinguishes explicitly defined upstreams from those that are automatically created by the [proxy\_pass](<085 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_proxy_module.md#proxy_pass>) directive and “friends” (FastCGI, SCGI, etc.)  
        `NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_CREATE` — 區分明確定義的上游伺服器與由 [proxy\_pass](<085 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_proxy_module.md#proxy_pass>)指令與「朋友」（FastCGI、 SCGI等）自動建立的上游伺服器。
    -   `NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_WEIGHT` — The “`weight`” parameter is supported  
        `NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_WEIGHT` — 支援「 `weight` 」參數
    -   `NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_MAX_FAILS` — The “`max_fails`” parameter is supported  
        `NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_MAX_FAILS` — 支援「 `max_fails` 」參數
    -   `NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_FAIL_TIMEOUT` — The “`fail_timeout`” parameter is supported  
        `NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_FAIL_TIMEOUT` — 支援「 `fail_timeout` 」參數
    -   `NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_DOWN` — The “`down`” parameter is supported  
        `NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_DOWN` — 支援「 `down` 」參數
    -   `NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_BACKUP` — The “`backup`” parameter is supported  
        `NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_BACKUP` — 支援「 `backup` 」參數
    -   `NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_MAX_CONNS` — The “`max_conns`” parameter is supported  
        `NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_MAX_CONNS` — 支援「 `max_conns` 」參數
-   `host` — Name of the upstream.  
    `host` — 上游名稱。
-   `file_name, line` — Name of the configuration file and the line where the `upstream` block is located.  
    `file_name, line` — 設定檔的名稱以及`upstream`程式碼區塊所在的行號。
-   `port` and `no_port` — Not used for explicitly defined upstream groups.  
    `port`和`no_port` — 不用於明確定義的上游群組。
-   `shm_zone` — Shared memory zone used by this upstream group, if any.  
    `shm_zone` — 此上游群組使用的共享記憶體區域（如果有）。
-   `peer` — object that holds generic methods for initializing upstream configuration:  
    `peer` — 包含用於初始化上游配置的通用方法的物件：
    
    > ```
    > typedef struct {
    >     ngx_http_upstream_init_pt        init_upstream;
    >
    >     ngx_http_upstream_init_pt init_upstream;
    >     ngx_http_upstream_init_peer_pt   init;
    >
    >     ngx_http_upstream_init_peer_pt 初始化;
    >     void                            *data;
    >
    >     void *data;
    > } ngx_http_upstream_peer_t;
    > ```
    
    A module that implements a load-balancing algorithm must set these methods and initialize private `data`. If `init_upstream` was not initialized during configuration parsing, `ngx_http_upstream_module` sets it to the default `ngx_http_upstream_init_round_robin` algorithm.

    實作負載平衡演算法的模組必須設定這些方法並初始化私有`data` 。如果在配置解析期間未初始化`init_upstream` ，則`ngx_http_upstream_module`將其設為預設`ngx_http_upstream_init_round_robin`演算法。
    -   `init_upstream(cf, us)` — Configuration-time method responsible for initializing a group of servers and initializing the `init()` method in case of success. A typical load-balancing module uses a list of servers in the `upstream` block to create an efficient data structure that it uses and saves its own configuration to the `data` field.  
        `init_upstream(cf, us)` — 配置時方法，負責初始化一組伺服器，並在成功時初始化`init()`方法。典型的負載平衡模組使用`upstream`程式碼區塊中的伺服器清單來建立高效的資料結構，並將自身的配置儲存到`data`欄位中。
    -   `init(r, us)` — Initializes a per-request `ngx_http_upstream_peer_t.peer` structure that is used for load balancing (not to be confused with the `ngx_http_upstream_srv_conf_t.peer` described above which is per-upstream). It is passed as the `data` argument to all callbacks that deal with server selection.  
        `init(r, us)` — 初始化一個用於負載平衡的請求級`ngx_http_upstream_peer_t.peer`結構（不要與上面描述的用於上游伺服器的`ngx_http_upstream_srv_conf_t.peer`混淆）。它會作為`data`參數傳遞給所有處理伺服器選擇的回呼函數。

When nginx has to pass a request to another host for processing, it uses the configured load-balancing method to obtain an address to connect to. The method is obtained from the `ngx_http_upstream_t.peer` object of type `ngx_peer_connection_t`:

當 nginx 需要將請求轉送到另一個主機進行處理時，它會使用設定的負載平衡方法來取得要連接的位址。此方法是從類型為`ngx_peer_connection_t`的`ngx_http_upstream_t.peer`物件中取得的：

> ```
> struct ngx_peer_connection_s {
>     ...
> 
>     struct sockaddr                 *sockaddr;
>
>     struct sockaddr *sockaddr;
>     socklen_t                        socklen;
>
>     socklen_t socklen;
>     ngx_str_t                       *name;
>
>     ngx_str_t *name;
> 
>     ngx_uint_t                       tries;
>
>     ngx_uint_t 嘗試；
> 
>     ngx_event_get_peer_pt            get;
>
>     ngx_event_get_peer_pt 取得；
>     ngx_event_free_peer_pt           free;
>
>     ngx_event_free_peer_pt 免費；
>     ngx_event_notify_peer_pt         notify;
>
>     ngx_event_notify_peer_pt 通知；
>     void                            *data;
>
>     void *data;
> 
> #if (NGX_SSL || NGX_COMPAT)
>     ngx_event_set_peer_session_pt    set_session;
>
>     ngx_event_set_peer_session_pt set_session;
>     ngx_event_save_peer_session_pt   save_session;
>
>     ngx_event_save_peer_session_pt save_session;
> #endif
> 
>     ...
> };
> ```

The structure has the following fields:

此結構包含以下欄位：

-   `sockaddr`, `socklen`, `name` — Address of the upstream server to connect to; this is the output parameter of a load-balancing method.  
    `sockaddr`, `socklen`, `name` — 要連接的上游伺服器的位址；這是負載平衡方法的輸出參數。
-   `data` — The per-request data of a load-balancing method; keeps the state of the selection algorithm and usually includes the link to the upstream configuration. It is passed as an argument to all methods that deal with server selection (see [below](#lb_method_get)).  
    `data` — 負載平衡方法的每次請求資料；保存選擇演算法的狀態，通常包含指向上游配置的連結。它作為參數傳遞給所有處理伺服器選擇的方法（參見[下文](#lb_method_get) ）。
-   `tries` — Allowed [number](<085 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_proxy_module.md#proxy_next_upstream_tries>) of attempts to connect to an upstream server.  
    `tries` — 允許 [number](<085 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_proxy_module.md#proxy_next_upstream_tries>)次嘗試連接到上游伺服器。
-   `get`, `free`, `notify`, `set_session`, and `save_session` - Methods of the load-balancing module, described below.  
    `get`, `free`, `notify`, `set_session`和`save_session` - 負載平衡模組的方法，如下所述。

All methods accept at least two arguments: a peer connection object `pc` and the `data` created by `ngx_http_upstream_srv_conf_t.peer.init()`. Note that it might differ from `pc.data` due to “chaining” of load-balancing modules.

所有方法至少接受兩個參數：一個對等連接物件`pc`和由`ngx_http_upstream_srv_conf_t.peer.init()`創建的`data` 。請注意，由於負載平衡模組的「鍊式」連接，它可能與`pc.data`不同。

-   `get(pc, data)` — The method called when the upstream module is ready to pass a request to an upstream server and needs to know its address. The method has to fill the `sockaddr`, `socklen`, and `name` fields of `ngx_peer_connection_t` structure. The return is one of:  
    `get(pc, data)` — 當上游模組準備好向上游伺服器發送請求並需要知道其位址時，將呼叫此方法。此方法必須填入`ngx_peer_connection_t`結構的`sockaddr`, `socklen`和`name`字段。傳回值為以下之一：
    -   `NGX_OK` — Server was selected.  
        `NGX_OK` — 已選擇伺服器。
    -   `NGX_ERROR` — Internal error occurred.  
        `NGX_ERROR` — 發生內部錯誤。
    -   `NGX_BUSY` — no servers are currently available. This can happen due to many reasons, including: the dynamic server group is empty, all servers in the group are in the failed state, or all servers in the group are already handling the maximum number of connections.  
        `NGX_BUSY` — 目前沒有可用伺服器。這可能是由多種原因造成的，包括：動態伺服器群組為空、群組內所有伺服器均處於故障狀態，或群組內所有伺服器已達到最大連線數。
    -   `NGX_DONE` — the underlying connection was reused and there is no need to create a new connection to the upstream server. This value is set by the `keepalive` module.  
        `NGX_DONE` — 底層連線已重複使用，無需與上游伺服器建立新連線。此值由`keepalive`模組設定。
-   `free(pc, data, state)` — The method called when an upstream module has finished work with a particular server. The `state` argument is the completion status of the upstream connection, a bitmask with the following possible values:  
    `free(pc, data, state)` — 當上游模組完成與特定伺服器的工作時所呼叫的方法。 `state`參數是上游連接的完成狀態，一個具有以下可能值的位元遮罩：
    
    -   `NGX_PEER_FAILED` — Attempt was [unsuccessful](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#max_fails>)  
        `NGX_PEER_FAILED` — 嘗試失敗(https://nginx.org/en/docs/http/ngx_http_upstream_module.html#max_fails)
    -   `NGX_PEER_NEXT` — A special case when upstream server returns codes `403` or `404`, which are not considered a [failure](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#max_fails>).  
        `NGX_PEER_NEXT` — 當上游伺服器回傳代碼`403`或`404`時，這是一個特殊情況，這些代碼不被視為 [失敗](<105 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_upstream_module.md#max_fails>) 。
    -   `NGX_PEER_KEEPALIVE` — Currently unused  
        `NGX_PEER_KEEPALIVE` — 目前未使用
    
    This method also decrements the `tries` counter.

    此方法也會遞減`tries`計數器。
-   `notify(pc, data, type)` — Currently unused in the OSS version.  
    `notify(pc, data, type)` — 目前在OSS版本中未使用。
-   `set_session(pc, data)` and `save_session(pc, data)` — SSL-specific methods that enable caching sessions to upstream servers. The implementation is provided by the round-robin balancing method.  
    `set_session(pc, data)`和`save_session(pc, data)` — SSL — 啟用會話快取到上游伺服器的特定方法。此實作由輪詢負載平衡方法提供。

#### Examples｜範例

The [nginx-dev-examples](https://github.com/nginx/nginx-dev-examples) repository provides nginx module examples.

[nginx-dev-examples](https://github.com/nginx/nginx-dev-examples)儲存庫提供 nginx 模組範例。

#### Code style｜程式碼風格

#### General rules｜一般規則

-   maximum text width is 80 characters  
    文字最大寬度為 80 個字符
-   indentation is 4 spaces  
    縮排為 4 個空格
-   no tabs, no trailing spaces  
    不要加製表符，不要加尾空格
-   list elements on the same line are separated with spaces  
    同一行上的列表元素以空格分隔。
-   hexadecimal literals are lowercase  
    十六進位文字是小寫的
-   file names, function and type names, and global variables have the `ngx_` or more specific prefix such as `ngx_http_` and `ngx_mail_`  
    檔案名稱、函數名稱、型別名稱和全域變數都帶有`ngx_`或更具體的前綴，例如`ngx_http_`和`ngx_mail_`

> ```
> size_t
> ngx_utf8_length(u_char *p, size_t n)
> {
>     u_char  c, *last;
>
>     u_char c，*last；
>     size_t  len;
>
>     size_t len;
> 
>     last = p + n;
> 
>     for (len = 0; p < last; len++) {
> 
>         c = *p;
> 
>         if (c < 0x80) {
>
>         如果 (c < 0x80) {
>             p++;
>             continue;
>
>             繼續;
>         }
> 
>         if (ngx_utf8_decode(&p, last - p) > 0x10ffff) {
>             /* invalid UTF-8 */
>
>             /* 無效UTF -8 */
>             return n;
>
>             返回 n；
>         }
>     }
> 
>     return len;
>
>     返回長度；
> }
> ```

#### Files｜文件

A typical source file may contain the following sections separated by two empty lines:

一個典型的來源檔案可能包含以下幾個部分，各部分之間以兩個空白行分隔：

-   copyright statements  
    版權聲明
-   includes  
    包括
-   preprocessor definitions  
    預處理器定義
-   type definitions  
    類型定義
-   function prototypes  
    函數原型
-   variable definitions  
    變數定義
-   function definitions  
    函數定義

Copyright statements look like this:

版權聲明如下圖所示：

> ```
> /*
>  * Copyright (C) Author Name  
     版權所有 (C) 作者姓名
>  * Copyright (C) Organization, Inc.  
     版權所有 (C) Organization, Inc.
>  */
> ```

If the file is modified significantly, the list of authors should be updated, the new author is added to the top.

如果文件進行了重大修改，則應更新作者列表，將新作者添加到列表頂部。

The `ngx_config.h` and `ngx_core.h` files are always included first, followed by one of `ngx_http.h`, `ngx_stream.h`, or `ngx_mail.h`. Then follow optional external header files:

首先引入`ngx_config.h`和`ngx_core.h`文件，然後是`ngx_http.h`, `ngx_stream.h`或`ngx_mail.h`之一。之後是可選的外部頭檔：

> ```
> #include <ngx_config.h>
>
> #包含<ngx_config.h>
> #include <ngx_core.h>
>
> #包含<ngx_core.h>
> #include <ngx_http.h>
>
> #包含<ngx_http.h>
> 
> #include <libxml/parser.h>
>
> #包含<libxml/parser.h>
> #include <libxml/tree.h>
>
> #包含<libxml/tree.h>
> #include <libxslt/xslt.h>
>
> #包含<libxslt/xslt.h>
> 
> #if (NGX_HAVE_EXSLT)
> #include <libexslt/exslt.h>
>
> #包含<libexslt/exslt.h>
> #endif
> ```

Header files should include the so called "header protection":

頭檔應包含所謂的「頭保護」：

> ```
> #ifndef _NGX_PROCESS_CYCLE_H_INCLUDED_
> #define _NGX_PROCESS_CYCLE_H_INCLUDED_
> ...
> #endif /* _NGX_PROCESS_CYCLE_H_INCLUDED_ */
> ```

#### Comments｜評論

-   “`//`” comments are not used  
    「 `//` 」評論不會被使用
-   text is written in English, American spelling is preferred  
    文字以英文撰寫，建議採用美式拼字。
-   multi-line comments are formatted like this:  
    多行註解的格式如下：
    
    > ```
    > /*
    >  * The red-black tree code is based on the algorithm described in  
         紅黑樹編碼基於以下演算法：
    >  * the "Introduction to Algorithms"（演算法導論） by Cormen, Leiserson and Rivest.  
         由 Cormen、Leiserson 和 Rivest 設計的"Introduction to Algorithms"（演算法導論） 。
    >  */
    > ```
    
    > ```
    > /* find the server configuration for the address:port */
    >
    > /* 查找位址:連接埠的伺服器配置 */
    > ```
    

#### Preprocessor｜預處理器

Macro names start from `ngx_` or `NGX_` (or more specific) prefix. Macro names for constants are uppercase. Parameterized macros and macros for initializers are lowercase. The macro name and value are separated by at least two spaces:

巨集名稱以`ngx_`或`NGX_` （或更具體的）前綴開頭。常量巨集名稱使用大寫字母。參數化巨集和初始化巨集使用小寫字母。巨集名稱和值之間至少用兩個空格分隔：

> ```
> #define NGX_CONF_BUFFER  4096
>
> #define NGX_CONF_BUFFER 4096
> 
> #define ngx_buf_in_memory(b)  (b->temporary || b->memory || b->mmap)
>
> #define ngx_buf_in_memory(b) (b->temporary || b->memory || b->mmap)
> 
> #define ngx_buf_size(b)                                                      \
>
> #define ngx_buf_size(b) \
>     (ngx_buf_in_memory(b) ? (off_t) (b->last - b->pos):                      \
>
>     (ngx_buf_in_memory(b) ? (off_t) (b->last - b->pos): \
>                             (b->file_last - b->file_pos))
> 
> #define ngx_null_string  { 0, NULL }
>
> #define ngx_null_string { 0, NULL }
> ```

Conditions are inside parentheses, negation is outside:

條件用括號括起來，否定用括號括起來：

> ```
> #if (NGX_HAVE_KQUEUE)
> ...
> #elif ((NGX_HAVE_DEVPOLL && !(NGX_TEST_BUILD_DEVPOLL)) \
>        || (NGX_HAVE_EVENTPORT && !(NGX_TEST_BUILD_EVENTPORT)))
> ...
> #elif (NGX_HAVE_EPOLL && !(NGX_TEST_BUILD_EPOLL))
> ...
> #elif (NGX_HAVE_POLL)
> ...
> #else /* select */
>
> #否則 /* 選擇 */
> ...
> #endif /* NGX_HAVE_KQUEUE */
> ```

#### Types｜類型

Type names end with the “`_t`” suffix. A defined type name is separated by at least two spaces:

類型名稱以“ `_t` ”後綴結尾。已定義的類型名稱之間至少以兩個空格分隔：

> ```
> typedef ngx_uint_t  ngx_rbtree_key_t;
>
> typedef ngx_uint_t ngx_rbtree_key_t;
> ```

Structure types are defined using `typedef`. Inside structures, member types and names are aligned:

結構類型使用`typedef`定義。在結構內部，成員類型和名稱保持一致：

> ```
> typedef struct {
>     size_t      len;
>
>     size_t len;
>     u_char     *data;
>
>     u_char *data;
> } ngx_str_t;
> ```

Keep alignment identical among different structures in the file. A structure that points to itself has the name, ending with “`_s`”. Adjacent structure definitions are separated with two empty lines:

保持文件中不同結構的對齊方式一致。指向自身的結構名稱以「 `_s` 」結尾。相鄰的結構定義之間以兩個空白行分隔：

> ```
> typedef struct ngx_list_part_s  ngx_list_part_t;
>
> typedef struct ngx_list_part_s ngx_list_part_t;
> 
> struct ngx_list_part_s {
>     void             *elts;
>
>     void *else;
>     ngx_uint_t        nelts;
>
>     ngx_uint_t nelts;
>     ngx_list_part_t  *next;
>
>     ngx_list_part_t *next;
> };
> 
> 
> typedef struct {
>     ngx_list_part_t  *last;
>
>     ngx_list_part_t *last;
>     ngx_list_part_t   part;
>
>     ngx_list_part_t part;
>     size_t            size;
>
>     size_t size;
>     ngx_uint_t        nalloc;
>
>     ngx_uint_t nalloc;
>     ngx_pool_t       *pool;
>
>     ngx_pool_t *pool;
> } ngx_list_t;
> ```

Each structure member is declared on its own line:

每個結構體成員都單獨佔一行：

> ```
> typedef struct {
>     ngx_uint_t        hash;
>
>     ngx_uint_t 哈希；
>     ngx_str_t         key;
>
>     ngx_str_t 鍵；
>     ngx_str_t         value;
>
>     ngx_str_t 值；
>     u_char           *lowcase_key;
>
>     u_char *lowcase_key;
> } ngx_table_elt_t;
> ```

Function pointers inside structures have defined types ending with “`_pt`”:

結構體內部的函數指標具有以「 `_pt` 」結尾的定義類型：

> ```
> typedef ssize_t (*ngx_recv_pt)(ngx_connection_t *c, u_char *buf, size_t size);
> typedef ssize_t (*ngx_recv_chain_pt)(ngx_connection_t *c, ngx_chain_t *in,
>     off_t limit);
>
>     off_t 限制);
> typedef ssize_t (*ngx_send_pt)(ngx_connection_t *c, u_char *buf, size_t size);
> typedef ngx_chain_t *(*ngx_send_chain_pt)(ngx_connection_t *c, ngx_chain_t *in,
>     off_t limit);
>
>     off_t 限制);
> 
> typedef struct {
>     ngx_recv_pt        recv;
>
>     ngx_recv_pt recv;
>     ngx_recv_chain_pt  recv_chain;
>
>     ngx_recv_chain_pt recv_chain;
>     ngx_recv_pt        udp_recv;
>
>     ngx_recv_pt udp_recv;
>     ngx_send_pt        send;
>
>     ngx_send_pt 發送；
>     ngx_send_pt        udp_send;
>
>     ngx_send_pt udp_send;
>     ngx_send_chain_pt  udp_send_chain;
>
>     ngx_send_chain_pt udp_send_chain;
>     ngx_send_chain_pt  send_chain;
>
>     ngx_send_chain_pt 發送鏈；
>     ngx_uint_t         flags;
>
>     ngx_uint_t 標誌；
> } ngx_os_io_t;
>
> ngx_os_io_t;
> ```

Enumerations have types ending with “`_e`”:

枚舉型別以「 `_e` 」結尾：

> ```
> typedef enum {
>     ngx_http_fastcgi_st_version = 0,
>     ngx_http_fastcgi_st_type,
>
>     ngx_http_fastcgi_st_type，
>     ...
>     ngx_http_fastcgi_st_padding
> } ngx_http_fastcgi_state_e;
> ```

#### Variables｜變數

Variables are declared sorted by length of a base type, then alphabetically. Type names and variable names are aligned. The type and name “columns” are separated with two spaces. Large arrays are put at the end of a declaration block:

變數宣告時，先依基本類型長度排序，再依字母順序排序。類型名和變數名對齊。類型和名稱「列」之間用兩個空格分隔。大型數組放在聲明區塊的末尾：

> ```js
> u_char                      |  | *rv, *p;
>
> u_char | | *rv, *p;
> ngx_conf_t                  |  | *cf;
>
> ngx_conf_t | | *cf;
> ngx_uint_t                  |  |  i, j, k;
>
> ngx_uint_t | | i, j, k;
> unsigned int                |  |  len;
>
> 無符號整數 | | len;
> struct sockaddr             |  | *sa;
>
> struct sockaddr | | *sa;
> const unsigned char         |  | *data;
>
> const unsigned char | | *data;
> ngx_peer_connection_t       |  | *pc;
>
> ngx_peer_connection_t | | *pc;
> ngx_http_core_srv_conf_t    |  |**cscfp;
>
> ngx_http_core_srv_conf_t | |**cscfp;
> ngx_http_upstream_srv_conf_t|  | *us, *uscf;
>
> ngx_http_upstream_srv_conf_t| | *us, *uscf;
> u_char                      |  |  text[NGX_SOCKADDR_STRLEN];
>
> u_char | | text[NGX_SOCKADDR_STRLEN];
> ```

Static and global variables may be initialized on declaration:

靜態變數和全域變數可以在宣告時進行初始化：

> ```
> static ngx_str_t  ngx_http_memcached_key = ngx_string("memcached_key");
>
> static ngx_str_t ngx_http_memcached_key = ngx_string("memcached_key");
> ```

> ```
> static ngx_uint_t  mday[] = { 31, 28, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31 };
>
> static ngx_uint_t mday[] = { 31, 28, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31 };
> ```

> ```
> static uint32_t  ngx_crc32_table16[] = {
>
> static uint32_t ngx_crc32_table16[] = {
>     0x00000000, 0x1db71064, 0x3b6e20c8, 0x26d930ac,
>     ...
>     0x9b64c2b0, 0x86d3d2d4, 0xa00ae278, 0xbdbdf21c
>
>     0x9b64c2b0、0x86d3d2d4、0xa00ae278、0xbdbdf21c
> };
> ```

There is a bunch of commonly used type/name combinations:

有很多常用的類型/名稱組合：

> ```
> u_char                        *rv;
>
> u_char *rv;
> ngx_int_t                      rc;
>
> ngx_int_t rc;
> ngx_conf_t                    *cf;
>
> ngx_conf_t *cf;
> ngx_connection_t              *c;
>
> ngx_connection_t *c;
> ngx_http_request_t            *r;
>
> ngx_http_request_t *r;
> ngx_peer_connection_t         *pc;
>
> ngx_peer_connection_t *pc;
> ngx_http_upstream_srv_conf_t  *us, *uscf;
>
> ngx_http_upstream_srv_conf_t *us, *uscf;
> ```

#### Functions｜函數

All functions (even static ones) should have prototypes. Prototypes include argument names. Long prototypes are wrapped with a single indentation on continuation lines:

所有函數（即使是靜態函數）都應該有函數原型。函數原型應包含參數名稱。較長的函數原型應在續行中使用單縮排：

> ```
> static char *ngx_http_block(ngx_conf_t *cf, ngx_command_t *cmd, void *conf);
> static ngx_int_t ngx_http_init_phases(ngx_conf_t *cf,
>     ngx_http_core_main_conf_t *cmcf);
> 
> static char *ngx_http_merge_servers(ngx_conf_t *cf,
>     ngx_http_core_main_conf_t *cmcf, ngx_http_module_t *module,
>
>     ngx_http_core_main_conf_t *cmcf，ngx_http_module_t *module，
>     ngx_uint_t ctx_index);
> ```

The function name in a definition starts with a new line. The function body opening and closing braces are on separate lines. The body of a function is indented. There are two empty lines between functions:

函數定義中的函數名稱以新行開頭。函數體（包括大括號和右大括號）分別位於不同的行上。函數體需要縮排。函數之間有兩行空行。

> ```
> static ngx_int_t
>
> 靜態 ngx_int_t
> ngx_http_find_virtual_server(ngx_http_request_t *r, u_char *host, size_t len)
> {
>     ...
> }
> 
> 
> static ngx_int_t
>
> 靜態 ngx_int_t
> ngx_http_add_addresses(ngx_conf_t *cf, ngx_http_core_srv_conf_t *cscf,
>     ngx_http_conf_port_t *port, ngx_http_listen_opt_t *lsopt)
> {
>     ...
> }
> ```

There is no space after the function name and opening parenthesis. Long function calls are wrapped such that continuation lines start from the position of the first function argument. If this is impossible, format the first continuation line such that it ends at position 79:

函數名和左括號後面沒有空格。較長的函數呼叫會被換行，使續行從第一個函數參數的位置開始。如果無法做到這一點，則將第一個續行格式化為在第 79 個位置結束：

> ```
> ngx_log_debug2(NGX_LOG_DEBUG_HTTP, r->connection->log, 0,
>                "http header: \"%V: %V\"",
>
>                "http 標頭：\"%V: %V\"",
>                &h->key, &h->value);
> 
> hc->busy = ngx_palloc(r->connection->pool,
>                   cscf->large_client_header_buffers.num * sizeof(ngx_buf_t *));
> ```

The `ngx_inline` macro should be used instead of `inline`:

應該使用`ngx_inline`宏，而不是`inline`宏：

> ```
> static ngx_inline void ngx_cpuid(uint32_t i, uint32_t *buf);
> ```

#### Expressions｜表達式

Binary operators except “`.`” and “`−>`” should be separated from their operands by one space. Unary operators and subscripts are not separated from their operands by spaces:

除「 `.` 」和「 `−>` 」之外的二元運算子應與其運算元之間以一個空格隔開。一元運算子和下標與其運算元之間不需要用空格隔開。

> ```
> width = width * 10 + (*fmt++ - '0');
>
> 寬度 = 寬度 * 10 + (*fmt++ - '0');
> ```

> ```
> ch = (u_char) ((decoded << 4) + (ch - '0'));
> ```

> ```
> r->exten.data = &r->uri.data[i + 1];
> ```

Type casts are separated by one space from casted expressions. An asterisk inside type cast is separated with space from type name:

類型轉換語句與被轉換表達式之間以一個空格隔開。類型轉換語句中的星號與型別名稱之間以空格隔開：

> ```
> len = ngx_sock_ntop((struct sockaddr *) sin6, p, len, 1);
> ```

If an expression does not fit into single line, it is wrapped. The preferred point to break a line is a binary operator. The continuation line is lined up with the start of expression:

如果表達式無法在一行內完整顯示，則會自動換行。換行的首選位置是二元運算子。續行與表達式的開頭對齊：

> ```
> if (status == NGX_HTTP_MOVED_PERMANENTLY
>
> 如果 (狀態 == NGX_HTTP_MOVED_PERMANENTLY
>     || status == NGX_HTTP_MOVED_TEMPORARILY
>
>     || 狀態 == NGX_HTTP_MOVED_TEMPORARILY
>     || status == NGX_HTTP_SEE_OTHER
>
>     || 狀態 == NGX_HTTP_SEE_OTHER
>     || status == NGX_HTTP_TEMPORARY_REDIRECT
>
>     || 狀態 == NGX_HTTP_TEMPORARY_REDIRECT
>     || status == NGX_HTTP_PERMANENT_REDIRECT)
>
>     || 狀態 == NGX_HTTP_PERMANENT_REDIRECT)
> {
>     ...
> }
> ```

> ```
> p->temp_file->warn = "an upstream response is buffered "
>
> p->temp_file->warn = "上游回應已緩衝"
>                      "to a temporary file";
>
>                      「儲存到臨時文件」；
> ```

As a last resort, it is possible to wrap an expression so that the continuation line ends at position 79:

作為最後的手段，可以對表達式進行換行，使續行結束於位置 79：

> ```
> hinit->hash = ngx_pcalloc(hinit->pool, sizeof(ngx_hash_wildcard_t)
>                                      + size * sizeof(ngx_hash_elt_t *));
> ```

The above rules also apply to sub-expressions, where each sub-expression has its own indentation level:

以上規則同樣適用於子表達式，其中每個子表達式都有自己的縮排等級：

> ```
> if (((u->conf->cache_use_stale & NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_FT_UPDATING)
>
> 如果 (((u->conf->cache_use_stale & NGX_HTTP_UPSTREAM_FT_UPDATING)
>      || c->stale_updating) && !r->background
>     && u->conf->cache_background_update)
> {
>     ...
> }
> ```

Sometimes, it is convenient to wrap an expression after a cast. In this case, the continuation line is indented:

有時，在型別轉換後將表達式換行會很方便。在這種情況下，續行會縮排：

> ```
> node = (ngx_rbtree_node_t *)
>
> 節點 = (ngx_rbtree_node_t *)
>            ((u_char *) lr - offsetof(ngx_rbtree_node_t, color));
> ```

Pointers are explicitly compared to `NULL` (not `0`):

指針會明確地與`NULL` （而非`0` ）進行比較：

> ```
> if (ptr != NULL) {
>
> 如果 (ptr != NULL ) {
>     ...
> }
> ```

#### Conditionals and Loops｜條件語句和迴圈語句

The “`if`” keyword is separated from the condition by one space. Opening brace is located on the same line, or on a dedicated line if the condition takes several lines. Closing brace is located on a dedicated line, optionally followed by “`else if` / `else`”. Usually, there is an empty line before the “`else if` / `else`” part:

「 `if` 」關鍵字與條件之間用一個空格隔開。左大括號位於同一行，如果條件佔多行，則左大括號位於單獨的一行。右大括號位於單獨的一行，其後可跟著「 `else if` / `else` 」。通常，在「 `else if` / `else` 」部分之前會有一個空白行。

> ```
> if (node->left == sentinel) {
>
> 如果 (node->left == sentinel) {
>     temp = node->right;
>     subst = node;
>
>     替換 = 節點；
> 
> } else if (node->right == sentinel) {
>     temp = node->left;
>     subst = node;
>
>     替換 = 節點；
> 
> } else {
>
> } 別的 {
>     subst = ngx_rbtree_min(node->right, sentinel);
> 
>     if (subst->left != sentinel) {
>
>     如果 (subst->left != sentinel) {
>         temp = subst->left;
> 
>     } else {
>
>     } 別的 {
>         temp = subst->right;
>     }
> }
> ```

Similar formatting rules are applied to “`do`” and “`while`” loops:

類似的格式規則也適用於「 `do` 」和「 `while` 」循環：

> ```
> while (p < last && *p == ' ') {
>     p++;
> }
> ```

> ```
> do {
>
> 做 {
>     ctx->node = rn;
>     ctx = ctx->next;
> } while (ctx);
> ```

The “`switch`” keyword is separated from the condition by one space. Opening brace is located on the same line. Closing brace is located on a dedicated line. The “`case`” keywords are lined up with “`switch`”:

「 `switch` 」關鍵字與條件之間用一個空格隔開。左大括號位於同一行。右大括號位於單獨的一行。 「 `case` 」關鍵字與「 `switch` 」對齊。

> ```
> switch (ch) {
>
> 開關（通道）{
> case '!':
>
> 案件 '！ '：
>     looked = 2;
>
>     看了一下 = 2；
>     state = ssi_comment0_state;
>
>     狀態 = ssi_comment0_state;
>     break;
>
>     休息;
> 
> case '<':
>     copy_end = p;
>     break;
>
>     休息;
> 
> default:
>
> 預設:
>     copy_end = p;
>     looked = 0;
>
>     已查看 = 0；
>     state = ssi_start_state;
>
>     狀態 = ssi_start_state;
>     break;
>
>     休息;
> }
> ```

Most “`for`” loops are formatted like this:

大多數「 `for` 」迴圈的格式如下：

> ```
> for (i = 0; i < ccf->env.nelts; i++) {
>     ...
> }
> ```

> ```
> for (q = ngx_queue_head(locations);
>      q != ngx_queue_sentinel(locations);
>      q = ngx_queue_next(q))
> {
>     ...
> }
> ```

If some part of the “`for`” statement is omitted, this is indicated by the “`/* void */`” comment:

如果省略了「 `for` 」語句的某些部分，則以「 `/* void */` 」註解表示：

> ```
> for (i = 0; /* void */ ; i++) {
>     ...
> }
> ```

A loop with an empty body is also indicated by the “`/* void */`” comment which may be put on the same line:

空循環體也可以用「 `/* void */` 」註解表示，註解可以放在同一行：

> ```
> for (cl = *busy; cl->next; cl = cl->next) { /* void */ }
> ```

An endless loop looks like this:

無限循環看起來像這樣：

> ```
> for ( ;; ) {
>
> 為了 （ ;; ） {
>     ...
> }
> ```

#### Labels｜標籤

Labels are surrounded with empty lines and are indented at the previous level:

標籤前後用空白行隔開，並且比上一層縮排：

> ```
>     if (i == 0) {
>
>     如果 (i == 0) {
>         u->err = "host not found";
>
>         u->err = "未找到主機";
>         goto failed;
>
>         goto 操作失敗；
>     }
> 
>     u->addrs = ngx_pcalloc(pool, i * sizeof(ngx_addr_t));
>     if (u->addrs == NULL) {
>
>     如果 (u->addrs == NULL ) {
>         goto failed;
>
>         goto 操作失敗；
>     }
> 
>     u->naddrs = i;
> 
>     ...
> 
>     return NGX_OK;
>
>     返回 NGX_OK；
> 
> failed:
>
> 失敗的：
> 
>     freeaddrinfo(res);
>     return NGX_ERROR;
>
>     返回 NGX_ERROR；
> ```

#### Debugging memory issues｜調試記憶體問題

To debug memory issues such as buffer overruns or use-after-free errors, you can use the [AddressSanitizer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AddressSanitizer) (ASan) supported by some modern compilers. To enable ASan with `gcc` and `clang`, use the `-fsanitize=address` compiler and linker option. When building nginx, this can be done by adding the option to `--with-cc-opt` and `--with-ld-opt` parameters of the `configure` script.

要調試諸如緩衝區溢位或釋放後使用錯誤之類的記憶體問題，可以使用一些現代編譯器支援的 [AddressSanitizer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AddressSanitizer) (ASan)。若要使用`gcc`和`clang`啟用 ASan，請使用`-fsanitize=address`編譯器和連結器選項。建構 nginx 時，可以透過將該選項新增至`--with-cc-opt`和`--with-ld-opt` `configure`的參數來實現。

Since most allocations in nginx are made from nginx internal [pool](#pool), enabling ASan may not always be enough to debug memory issues. The internal pool allocates a big chunk of memory from the system and cuts smaller allocations from it. However, this mechanism can be disabled by setting the `NGX_DEBUG_PALLOC` macro to `1`. In this case, allocations are passed directly to the system allocator giving it full control over the buffers boundaries.

由於 nginx 中的大多數記憶體分配都來自 nginx 內部的 [pool](#pool) ，因此啟用 ASan 可能不足以偵錯記憶體問題。內部記憶體池會從系統中分配一大塊內存，然後從中分割出較小的內存進行分配。但是，可以將`NGX_DEBUG_PALLOC`宏設定為`1`來停用此機制。在這種情況下，記憶體分配將直接傳遞給系統分配器，使其能夠完全控制緩衝區邊界。

The following configuration line summarizes the information provided above. It is recommended while developing third-party modules and testing nginx on different platforms.

以下配置行總結了上述資訊。建議在開發第三方模組和在不同平台上測試 nginx 時使用此設定行。

> ```
> auto/configure --with-cc-opt='-fsanitize=address -DNGX_DEBUG_PALLOC=1'
>                --with-ld-opt=-fsanitize=address
>
>                --with-ld-opt=-fsanitize=地址
> ```

#### Common Pitfalls｜常見陷阱

#### Writing a C module｜編寫 C 模組

The most common pitfall is an attempt to write a full-fledged C module when it can be avoided. In most cases your task can be accomplished by creating a proper configuration. If writing a module is inevitable, try to make it as small and simple as possible. For example, a module can only export some [variables](#http_variables).

最常見的陷阱是試圖編寫一個完整的 C 模組，而實際上可以避免。大多數情況下，透過建立合適的配置即可完成任務。如果編寫模組不可避免，請盡量使其盡可能小巧簡潔。例如，一個模組可以只(https://nginx.org/en/docs/dev/development_guide.html#http_variables)一些變數。

Before starting a module, consider the following questions:

在開始學習某個模組之前，請先考慮以下問題：

-   Is it possible to implement a desired feature using already [available modules](<114 [01.01 文件] nginx 文檔.md>)?  
    是否可以使用已有的[可用模組](<114 [01.01 文件] nginx 文檔.md>)來實現所需的功能？
-   Is it possible to solve an issue using built-in scripting languages, such as [Perl](<084 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_perl_module.md>) or [njs](<133 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] nginx JavaScript 模組.md>)?  
    是否可以使用內建腳本語言（例如 [Perl](<084 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_perl_module.md>)或 [njs](<133 [01.01.05 紐澤西州] nginx JavaScript 模組.md>) ）來解決問題？

#### C Strings｜C 字串

The most used string type in nginx, [ngx\_str\_t](#string_overview) is not a C-Style zero-terminated string. You cannot pass the data to standard C library functions such as `strlen()` or `strstr()`. Instead, nginx [counterparts](#string_overview) that accept either `ngx_str_t` should be used or pointer to data and a length. However, there is a case when `ngx_str_t` holds a pointer to a zero-terminated string: strings that come as a result of configuration file parsing are zero-terminated.

nginx 中最常用的字串型別 [ngx\_str\_t](#string_overview)不是 C 風格的以零結尾的字串。您不能將資料傳遞給標準 C 函式庫函數，例如`strlen()`或`strstr()` 。相反，應該使用 nginx 的對應函數(https://nginx.org/en/docs/dev/development_guide.html#string_overview) ，這些函數接受`ngx_str_t`或指向資料的指標以及長度。但是，有一種情況`ngx_str_t`持有指向以零結尾的字串的指標：設定檔解析產生的字串是以零結尾的。

#### Global Variables｜全域變數

Avoid using global variables in your modules. Most likely this is an error to have a global variable. Any global data should be tied to a [configuration cycle](#cycle) and be allocated from the corresponding [memory pool](#pool). This allows nginx to perform graceful configuration reloads. An attempt to use global variables will likely break this feature, because it will be impossible to have two configurations at the same time and get rid of them. Sometimes global variables are required. In this case, special attention is needed to manage reconfiguration properly. Also, check if libraries used by your code have implicit global state that may be broken on reload.

避免在模組中使用全域變數。使用全域變數很可能是個錯誤。任何全域資料都應該綁定到[配置週期](#cycle) ，並從對應的[記憶體池](#pool)中分配。這樣可以使 nginx 執行優雅的設定重載。嘗試使用全域變數很可能會破壞此功能，因為無法同時存在兩個配置並清除它們。有時確實需要使用全域變數。在這種情況下，需要特別注意正確管理重配置。此外，還要檢查程式碼中使用的庫是否具有可能在重載時被破壞的隱式全域狀態。

#### Manual Memory Management｜手動記憶體管理

Instead of dealing with malloc/free approach which is error prone, learn how to use nginx [pools](#pool). A pool is created and tied to an object - [configuration](#http_conf), [cycle](#cycle), [connection](#connection), or [HTTP request](#http_request). When the object is destroyed, the associated pool is destroyed too. So when working with an object, it is possible to allocate the amount needed from the corresponding pool and don't care about freeing memory even in case of errors.

與其使用容易出錯的 malloc/free 方法，不如學習如何使用 nginx 的 [記憶體池](#pool) 。記憶體池會建立一個並與物件綁定，例如 [配置](#http_conf) 、[循環](#cycle) 、[連接](#connection)或 [HTTP請求](#http_request) 。當物件被銷毀時，關聯的記憶體池也會被銷毀。因此，在使用物件時，可以從對應的記憶體池中分配所需的記憶體量，即使發生錯誤也不用擔心記憶體釋放的問題。

#### Threads｜執行緒

It is recommended to avoid using threads in nginx because it will definitely break things: most nginx functions are not thread-safe. It is expected that a thread will be executing only system calls and thread-safe library functions. If you need to run some code that is not related to client request processing, the proper way is to schedule a timer in the `init_process` module handler and perform required actions in timer handler. Internally nginx makes use of [threads](#threads) to boost IO-related operations, but this is a special case with a lot of limitations.

建議避免在 Nginx 中使用線程，因為這肯定會導致問題：大多數 Nginx 函數都不是線程安全的。線程應該只執行系統呼叫和線程安全的庫函數。如果您需要執行一些與用戶端請求處理無關的程式碼，正確的方法是在模組處理程序中安排一個計時器，並在`init_process`處理程序中執行所需的操作。 Nginx 內部會使用執行緒來加速與客戶IO (https://nginx.org/en/docs/dev/development_guide.html#threads)處理相關的操作，但這是存在許多限制的特殊情況。

#### Blocking Libraries｜阻塞庫

A common mistake is to use libraries that are blocking internally. Most libraries out there are synchronous and blocking by nature. In other words, they perform one operation at a time and waste time waiting for response from other peer. As a result, when a request is processed with such library, whole nginx worker is blocked, thus destroying performance. Use only libraries that provide asynchronous interface and don't block whole process.

一個常見的錯誤是使用內部阻塞的函式庫。大多數庫本質上都是同步的，並且會阻塞。換句話說，它們一次只能執行一個操作，並且會浪費時間等待其他節點的回應。因此，當使用這樣的程式庫處理請求時，整個 Nginx 工作進程都會被阻塞，從而嚴重影響效能。請僅使用提供非同步介面且不會阻塞整個進程的程式庫。

#### HTTP Requests to External Services｜HTTP向外部服務機構提出的請求

Often modules need to perform an HTTP call to some external service. A common mistake is to use some external library, such as libcurl, to perform the HTTP request. It is absolutely unnecessary to bring a huge amount of external (probably [blocking](#using_libraries)!) code for the task which can be accomplished by nginx itself.

模組經常需要向外部服務發出HTTP 。一個常見的錯誤是使用外部程式庫（例如 libcurl）來執行此請求。實際上， HTTP本身就能完成這項任務，完全沒有必要引入大量外部程式碼（而且很可能是阻塞(https://nginx.org/en/docs/dev/development_guide.html#using_libraries)的！）。

There are two basic usage scenarios when an external request is needed:

當需要進行外部請求時，主要有兩種使用情境：

-   in the context of processing a client request (for example, in content handler)  
    在處理客戶端請求的上下文中（例如，在內容處理程序中）
-   in the context of a worker process (for example, timer handler)  
    在工作進程（例如，定時器處理程序）的上下文中

In the first case, the best is to use [subrequests API](#http_subrequests). Instead of directly accessing external service, you declare a location in nginx configuration and direct your subrequest to this location. This location is not limited to [proxying](<085 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_proxy_module.md#proxy_pass>) requests, but may contain other nginx directives. An example of such approach is the [auth\_request](<051 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_auth_request_module.md#auth_request>) directive implemented in [ngx\_http\_auth\_request module](https://github.com/nginx/nginx/blob/master/src/http/modules/ngx_http_auth_request_module.c).

第一種情況下，最佳方法是使用 [子請求API](#http_subrequests) 。您無需直接存取外部服務，而是在 nginx 配置中聲明一個 location，並將子請求定向到該 location。此 location 不限於 [代理](<085 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_proxy_module.md#proxy_pass>)請求，還可以包含其他 nginx 指令。這種方法的一個例子是 [ngx_http_auth_request 模組](https://github.com/nginx/nginx/blob/master/src/http/modules/ngx_http_auth_request_module.c)中實作的 [auth_request](<051 [01.01.03 http] 模組 ngx_http_auth_request_module.md#auth_request>)指令。

For the second case, it is possible to use basic HTTP client functionality available in nginx. For example, [OCSP module](https://github.com/nginx/nginx/blob/master/src/event/ngx_event_openssl_stapling.c) implements simple HTTP client.

對於第二種情況，可以使用 nginx 中提供的基本HTTP端功能。例如，[OCSP模組](https://github.com/nginx/nginx/blob/master/src/event/ngx_event_openssl_stapling.c)實作了簡單的HTTP端。
